### [1] [Evidence-Order Calibration for Selective Visual Reasoning under Progressive Loss of Question-Critical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09184)
*Muhamathu Ameer Ali Aacaas Muhamath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究VLM沿证据损失轨迹的答案级可靠性，发现原生置信度存在严重证据单调性违背（EMVR=0.436），训练轻量事后可靠性头并加入证据顺序监督可将掩码场景EMVR从0.330降至0.303，在未见高斯模糊下从0.449降至0.402，但正确性判别指标差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉证据退化时，VLM置信度在总体上可能变化，但在单个样本内部却保持结构不一致。作者希望区分"证据顺序一致性"与传统的"正确性判别"，检验置信度是否随证据逐步缺失而单调变化。

Method: 使用冻结的Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct模型，基于GQA构建176条被接受的五步、问题条件化证据损失轨迹（880个掩码条件），逐步掩蔽场景图定位的问题关键区域并设非关键区域对照。随后在冻结隐状态、序列置信度和熵上训练轻量事后可靠性头，在BCE上加入证据顺序监督。

Result: 原生序列置信度的EMVR为0.436，92.0%轨迹至少含一次相邻违背。完全掩蔽关键区域使准确率下降28.2个百分点，等量非关键掩蔽仅降0.6点，配对差27.6点（95% CI [20.0, 34.7]）。加入证据顺序监督后EMVR从0.330降至0.303（配对差-0.027，95% CI [-0.044, -0.010]）；同一掩码训练目标在未见高斯模糊下将EMVR从0.449降至0.402（差-0.0468，95% CI [-0.0739, -0.0199]）。两学习头的AUROC、Brier、AURC差异统计上不显著，原生置信度在选择性风险排序上仍更强。

Conclusion: 结果将证据顺序一致性与传统正确性判别区分开来，而非证明通用置信度优越性；证据顺序监督能改善单调性，但不提升正确性判别能力。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) confidence may change in aggregate when visual evidence is degraded while remaining structurally inconsistent within individual examples. We study answer-level reliability along five-step, question-conditioned evidence-loss trajectories. Using a frozen Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct model, we construct 176 accepted GQA-derived trajectories (880 masking conditions) by progressively masking scene-graph-localized question-critical regions. Native sequence confidence has an evidence monotonicity violation rate (EMVR) of 0.436, and 92.0% of trajectories contain at least one adjacent violation. A matched non-critical-region control shows that full critical masking reduces accuracy by 28.2 percentage points, compared with 0.6 points for equally sized non-critical masks; the paired difference is 27.6 points (95% CI [20.0, 34.7]). We train a lightweight post-hoc reliability head on frozen hidden states, sequence confidence, and entropy. Adding evidence-order supervision to binary cross-entropy (BCE) reduces masking EMVR from 0.330 to 0.303 (paired difference -0.027, 95% CI [-0.044, -0.010]). The same mask-trained objective reduces EMVR from 0.449 to 0.402 on held-out question IDs under unseen local Gaussian blur (difference -0.0468, 95% CI [-0.0739, -0.0199]). AUROC, Brier, and AURC differences between the two learned heads are statistically inconclusive, and native confidence remains stronger for selective-risk ranking. The results separate evidence-order consistency from conventional correctness discrimination rather than establishing generic confidence superiority.

</details>


### [2] [Integrating Unimodal and Vision-Language Representations in Latent Space for Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09185)
*Quang-Huy Tran,Duc-Tuan Ngo,Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Bui,Dang-Khoa Bui,Thanh-Trong Tran,Tuan-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出融合RAD-DINO单模态视觉表征与BioViL-T视觉-语言表征的框架，用于MIMIC-CXR-JPG的14类多标签胸片分类，最优模型平均AUROC达0.840、mAP达0.467。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签胸片分类中视觉表征与临床语义知识的有效利用至关重要，但不同来源嵌入各自的作用及其互补程度尚不清晰，本文旨在提升分类性能的同时厘清各嵌入来源的贡献。

Method: 框架在RAD-DINO嵌入、BioViL-T嵌入及二者组合表示三条分支上分别于隐空间进行精炼，随后归一化并融合；同时开展消融分析，对比早期融合与混合融合的效果。

Result: 单独使用时RAD-DINO优于BioViL-T；早期融合可进一步提升性能，说明两种嵌入含互补信息；最优模型平均AUROC为0.840、mAP为0.467。消融显示，在各嵌入于隐空间精炼后，混合融合相较早期融合带来一致且统计显著的提升。

Conclusion: 融合效果取决于各分支所提供表征的质量；本研究仅在MIMIC-CXR-JPG上完成内部评估，对其他医疗机构数据的泛化性仍待验证。

Abstract: Multi-label chest X-ray classification has attracted considerable attention in recent years, with the effective use of visual representations and clinical semantic knowledge playing an important role. This study proposes a framework that combines unimodal representations from RAD-DINO with vision--language representations from BioViL-T for the classification of 14 labels in the MIMIC-CXR-JPG dataset. The RAD-DINO and BioViL-T embeddings and their combined representation are refined separately in latent space before being normalized and fused across the three branches. In addition to improving classification performance, the study aims to clarify the role of each embedding source and the degree to which they complement one another.
  Experiments show that RAD-DINO outperforms BioViL-T when used independently, whereas early fusion further improves the results, indicating that the two embedding sources contain complementary information. The best-performing model achieves a mean AUROC of 0.840 and an mAP of 0.467. Ablation analysis shows that hybrid fusion provides consistent and statistically significant improvements over early fusion when each embedding source is refined in latent space, suggesting that fusion effectiveness depends on the quality of the representation supplied by each branch. However, the study has only been evaluated internally on MIMIC-CXR-JPG; its generalizability to data from other healthcare institutions therefore remains to be validated. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mimic-report-C210/.

</details>


### [3] [M2LG-DG: A Multi-modal Local-Global Domain Generalization Framework for Cross-site Major Depressive Disorder Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09186)
*Muhammad Asif Hasan,Yanming Zhu,Xuefei Yin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M2LG-DG：仅用源站数据的多模态局部-全局域泛化框架，用于跨站点MDD分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rs-fMRI分类模型在未参与开发的成像站点性能下降，限制临床应用；现有域泛化方法多依赖单一模态，且未充分考虑站点特异采集效应对表征空间的影响（同站点受试者共享扫描仪、采集设置与预处理特性，导致表征反映采集条件而非诊断信息）。

Method: M2LG-DG为source-only多模态局部-全局框架：双流rs-fMRI编码器，全局路径用自注意力建模区域间依赖，局部路径在功能连接导出的脑图上进行图约束聚合；将影像与非影像表征分解为共享与私有分量，通过双向交叉注意力结合可学习模态门控进行融合；采用跨站点监督对比目标，将不同源站点同类受试者构成正样本对，促使融合表征跨采集域保留诊断信息。

Result: 在四个留出的REST-meta-MDD站点上，AUC达69.48%，较最接近的对比方法高出2.18个百分点；在ABIDE数据集上的实验进一步支持其适用于其他精神疾病神经影像分类任务。

Conclusion: M2LG-DG通过多模态共享-私有分解与跨站点对比学习，有效缓解站点采集效应，提升跨站点MDD分类的泛化性能，并具备向其他精神疾病神经影像任务推广的潜力。

Abstract: Classification models based on resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) often show lower performance at imaging sites not included during model development, which can limit their use in clinical settings. Domain generalization (DG) addresses this issue by learning representations from source sites that remain effective for unseen target sites. However, existing DG approaches for psychiatric disorder classification commonly rely on a single imaging modality and may not fully account for site-specific acquisition effects on the learned representation space. Subjects scanned at the same site share scanner hardware, acquisition settings, and preprocessing characteristics, which can cause representations to reflect acquisition conditions rather than diagnostic information. In this work, we present M2LG-DG, a source-only multimodal local-global framework for cross-site major depressive disorder (MDD) classification. The framework employs a dual-stream rs-fMRI encoder, where the global pathway models inter-regional dependencies through self-attention and the local pathway performs graph-constrained aggregation over functional connectivity-derived brain graphs. Imaging and non-imaging representations are decomposed into shared and private components and integrated through bidirectional cross-attention with a learned modality gate. A cross-site supervised contrastive objective forms positive pairs from same-class subjects acquired at different source sites, encouraging the fused representation to preserve diagnostic information across acquisition domains. On four held-out REST-meta-MDD sites, M2LG-DG achieves an AUC of 69.48% and exceeds the closest comparison method by 2.18 percentage points. Experiments on the Autism Brain Imaging Data Exchange (ABIDE) dataset further support its applicability to other psychiatric neuroimaging classification tasks.

</details>


### [4] [AgenticGen: Reward-Guided Agentic Video Generation for Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09187)
*Xingyuan Bu,Chengru Song,Hao Zhou,Tao Zhou,Dong Li,Wei Li,Shilong Li,Hao Shi,Yongxin Guo,Donghao Zhou,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgenticGen，一个奖励引导的智能体框架，将广告视频生成拆解为策略选择与草稿生成两个可训练推理阶段，利用在线业务反馈学习奖励并进行策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型虽能根据多模态条件生成逼真视频，但未优化"如何将产品转化为有效广告"，也未利用在线业务反馈来改进未来生成，导致生成目标与商业效果脱节。

Method: 将广告视频生成视为产品条件推理问题，分解为策略选择与草稿生成两个可训练阶段；从累积在线反馈学习基于表现的奖励，并引入与人类质量标准对齐的评分表奖励；先用 DPO 使策略趋向在线偏好，再用 GRPO 结合过程奖励与结果奖励进一步精炼两个阶段。

Result: 离线实验验证了奖励模型的有效性及逐步策略优化的效果；TikTok 广告系统的在线 A/B 实验显示，经 DPO 与 GRPO 训练后，相比 SFT 基线 CTR 提升 2.72%、CVR 提升 2.63%、Advv 提升 9.61%。

Conclusion: 通过将广告视频生成建模为奖励引导的两阶段智能体推理任务，并以在线业务指标监督优化，AgenticGen 成功打通了生成质量与商业效果的闭环，显著提升了实际广告系统的关键业务指标。

Abstract: Advertising video generation is not only a video synthesis task, but also a product-conditioned reasoning problem whose success is measured by online business metrics. Recent video foundation models can generate realistic clips from multimodal conditions, yet they do not optimize how a product should be transformed into an effective advertisement or how future generation should be improved from online business feedback. To close this loop, we propose AgenticGen, a reward-guided agentic framework that decomposes advertising video generation into two trainable reasoning stages, strategy selection and draft generation, thereby exposing optimization targets that online business feedback can supervise. AgenticGen learns a performance-based reward from accumulated online feedback and a complementary rubric-based reward aligned with human quality standards, then uses them to supervise policy optimization. DPO first moves the agentic policies toward online preferences, and GRPO further refines both stages with process and outcome rewards. Offline experiments validate the reward models and successive policy optimization. Online A/B experiments in the TikTok advertising system show that AgenticGen after DPO and GRPO improves CTR by 2.72%, CVR by 2.63%, and Advv by 9.61% over the SFT baseline.

</details>


### [5] [Lensless Gaze Is Not Private by Default: Auditing Identity Leakage Across Disclosure Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09188)
*Rahul Vimalkanth,Kaushik Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 无透镜近眼感知的"视觉不可理解"不等于身份隐私安全；在36人闭集识别协议下，无透镜测量仍可实现96.7%的top-1身份恢复，压缩与令牌化仅部分降低风险，隐私须在披露边界上实测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无透镜近眼感知因编码测量在视觉上不可解读而被普遍宣称为隐私友好，但视觉不可理解性反映的是人类解读能力，而非学习型攻击者可恢复的信息量。作者因此主张将身份隐私视为披露面（感知、存储、计算、输出边界）上的系统属性，并对无透镜注视管线进行隐私审计。

Method: 在固定且已知PSF的模拟无透镜注视管线上，采用36名受试者的已知库闭集识别协议；使用匹配的线性探针与MLP探针评估经验攻击成功率。比较对象包括原始眼部裁剪、MAE嵌入、8维PCA、匹配的8维瓶颈、分别训练的8维GSPL瓶颈（3个随机种子），以及128路注视令牌、其残差与连续注视输出。另设源帧不相交的分块协议，评估T=25时的令牌摘要聚合风险；并用普通最小二乘对六维裁剪几何与强度摘要做残差化以检验混杂信息贡献。

Result: 模拟无透镜测量的top-1识别率为96.7%，匹配的原始眼部裁剪为97.7%，MAE嵌入保留94.3%。仅压缩防护有限：8维PCA与匹配8维瓶颈分别保留93.2%和91.8%，而分别训练的8维GSPL瓶颈在三种子下平均恢复率降至77.5%。发布128路注视令牌使单帧恢复降至38.1%，但其残差与连续注视输出分别暴露62.1%和72.6%。分块协议下T=25时令牌摘要达39.9%，说明重复输出风险取决于表示与聚合方式。对六维裁剪几何与强度摘要做OLS残差化后，无透镜恢复仍为95.1%。

Conclusion: 无透镜感知的隐私主张必须在真实披露边界上实测，而不能由外观不可解读性推断。所报告比率反映评估数据集中全部与受试者相关的信息（含采集与行为线索），并非仅隔离固有眼动生物特征；结果亦不构成对更强攻击者的上界。

Abstract: Lensless near-eye sensing is often described as privacy-friendly because its coded measurements are visually unintelligible. Yet visual unintelligibility reflects human interpretation, not what a learned adversary can recover. We therefore treat identity privacy as a systems property of disclosure surfaces: representations crossing sensing, storage, computation, and output boundaries. We audit a simulated lensless gaze pipeline under a 36-subject known-gallery closed-set identification protocol with a fixed, known PSF; privacy from an unknown or varying optical key is outside our scope. Reported accuracies are empirical attack success rates under matched linear and MLP probes and do not upper-bound stronger adversaries. Simulated lensless measurements yield 96.7% top-1 identification versus 97.7% for matched original eye crops, while an MAE embedding retains 94.3%. Compression alone offers little protection: 8-D PCA and a matched 8-D bottleneck retain 93.2% and 91.8%, whereas separately trained 8-D GSPL bottlenecks yield 77.5% mean recovery across three seeds. A released 128-way gaze token lowers single-frame recovery to 38.1%, while its residual and continuous gaze output expose 62.1% and 72.6%, respectively. Under a source-frame-disjoint tiled protocol, token summaries reach 39.9% at T=25, showing that repeated-output risk depends on representation and aggregation. These rates reflect all subject-correlated information in the evaluated dataset, including acquisition and behavioral cues, rather than isolating intrinsic ocular biometrics. Ordinary least squares residualization against a six-dimensional crop geometry and intensity summary still leaves lensless recovery at 95.1%. Our results show that privacy claims for lensless sensing must be tested at disclosure boundaries rather than inferred from appearance.

</details>


### [6] [MLLMs Hallucinate when Information Distribution Drifts in Synergy Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09206)
*Meng'en Qin,Junye Chen,Jucheng Liu,Youlu Xing,Song Wang,Ruize Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HEAL，一种基于注意力头层级信息解耦与校准的方法，用于识别并缓解多模态大模型的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型的基于注意力的幻觉缓解方法主要依赖间接信号（如注意力权重），无法准确反映幻觉生成背后的真实信息偏移，因此缓解效果受限。

Method: HEAL首先对多头输出施加因果噪声干预以过滤掉因果冗余的注意力头；随后通过反事实双重差分（Difference-in-Differences）解耦剩余头内的信息分布，将头分为四类；再向协同头的value向量注入动态信息校准因子，主动调节视觉-语言依赖关系，将输出分布引导向事实证据。

Result: 分析发现：幻觉发生在协同头中信息分布偏离健康均衡时，与模态特定头的数量或强度并非强相关。大量实验表明HEAL在多个MLLM上有效降低幻觉。

Conclusion: HEAL提供了一条简单且可解释的路径来提升多模态大模型的可信度。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with hallucinations, thus hindering their reliable practical applications. Existing attention-based mitigation methods mainly rely on indirect signals (e.g., attention weights) that fail to accurately reflect the actual information shift underlying hallucination generation. In this paper, we propose HEAL, Head-lEvel information disentAnglement and caLibration for identifying and mitigating hallucinations. HEAL first employs causal noise intervention on multi-head outputs to filter out causally redundant heads. Subsequently, it disentangles information distribution within the remaining heads via the counterfactual Difference-in-Differences, categorizing heads into four types. Through analysis, we observe: hallucinations happen when information distribution drifts away from a healthy equilibrium in synergy heads, not strongly correlated with the quantity or strength of modality-specific heads. Motivated by this insight, HEAL injects dynamic information calibration factors into the value vectors of synergy heads, and actively regulates visual-language dependencies, steering the output distribution towards factual evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HEAL effectively reduces hallucinations across multiple MLLMs, offering a simple and interpretable pathway to enhance model trustworthiness.

</details>


### [7] [Video-MOPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09300)
*Zhenxin Qin,Peng Shi,Cong Han,Yinlong Qian,Zequn Jie,Lin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video-MOPD-8B，通过多领域强化学习与多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）及可靠性感知信息采样（RAIS），在视频理解多任务上达到同规模最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解需同时具备感知、时序理解与复杂推理能力，单一模型难以联合优化这些互补能力，因此需要针对性增强并结合多能力。

Method: 先针对视频时序定位（VTG）、通用视频理解、视频STEM推理三个核心领域分别进行强化学习优化；再用多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）以路由教师反馈监督学生生成轨迹，融合专家知识；并提出可靠性感知信息采样（RAIS），挑选教师监督一致可靠且师生性能差距大的样本。

Result: 在通用视频理解、时序定位、视频推理和视频STEM等综合基准上，Video-MOPD-8B在同规模模型中达到SOTA性能；模型权重已开源。

Conclusion: 多领域RL结合多教师蒸馏与可靠性采样，可实现多种视频理解能力的协同与全面提升，形成开源的高性能视频理解模型。

Abstract: Video understanding demands a convergence of complementary capabilities across perception, temporal understanding, and complex reasoning, which are difficult to jointly optimize within a single model. We introduce Video-MOPD-8B, an open-weight model dedicated to video understanding tasks. To fundamentally enhance its capabilities, we conduct targeted reinforcement learning (RL) optimization across three core domains: video temporal grounding (VTG), general video comprehension, and video STEM reasoning. We then unify their complementary capabilities via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), which consolidates expert knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with routed teacher feedback. We further introduce Reliability-Aware Informative Sampling (RAIS), which selects examples with consistently reliable teacher supervision and large teacher-student performance gaps. Together, these components enable Video-MOPD-8B to achieve coordinated and comprehensive performance gains across diverse video understanding tasks. Extensive experiments on comprehensive benchmarks covering general video understanding, temporal grounding, video reasoning, and video STEM tasks demonstrate that Video-MOPD-8B achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing models at a comparable scale. The trained model weights are available at https://huggingface.co/LandH/Video-MOPD-8B.

</details>


### [8] [DensePol: Dense-Angle Polarization Dataset for Learning-Based Polarimetric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09359)
*Param Sangani,Ahmad Moori,Erik Blasch,Guna Seetharaman,Hadi Aliakbarpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DensePol，一个基于分时（DoT）采集的高冗余RGB-偏振数据集，包含180个1°间隔的检偏器方向和2018对全分辨率图像；并提出确定性扩散框架（循环AoLP表示+局部DoLP细化器），提升偏振预测与下游表面法线估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振视觉可提供RGB难以恢复的场景形状、材质与反射物理线索，近期研究尝试从RGB直接预测偏振，但其保真度强烈依赖训练所用的偏振监督。现有数据集多基于DoFP相机，仅有四个空间交错检偏器方向，角度冗余有限，并引入插值与瞬时视场误差。

Method: 1) 构建DensePol数据集：基于分时（DoT）采集，获取180个全分辨率检偏器方向（1°间隔），共2018对RGB-偏振图像，保留角度测量与拟合残差。2) 提出确定性扩散式RGB到偏振框架，采用循环AoLP表示，并引入局部DoLP细化器。

Result: 密集角度采样显著提升偏振稳定性，将AoLP偏差从13.36°降至2.21°；实验表明偏振预测与下游表面法线估计均有改善。

Conclusion: DensePol提供了高角度冗余的高质量RGB-偏振监督数据，配合确定性扩散框架有效提升偏振预测性能，数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: Polarimetric vision is gaining increasing attention because it provides physical cues about scene shape, material, and reflection that are difficult to recover from RGB alone. Recent work has therefore explored predicting polarization directly from conventional RGB images; however, the fidelity of these methods strongly depends on the polarization supervision used for training. Most existing datasets rely on Division-of-Focal-Plane (DoFP) cameras with four spatially interleaved analyzer orientations, which provide limited angular redundancy and introduce interpolation and instantaneous-field-of-view errors. We introduce DensePol, a high-redundancy RGB--polarization dataset based on Division-of-Time (DoT) acquisition, capturing 180 full-resolution analyzer orientations at $1^\circ$ intervals. DensePol contains 2,018 paired RGB--polarization images with the angular measurements and fitting residuals retained. Dense angular sampling substantially improves polarization stability, reducing AoLP deviation from $13.36^\circ$ to $2.21^\circ$. We further introduce a deterministic diffusion-based RGB-to-polarization framework with cyclic AoLP representation and a local DoLP refiner. Experiments demonstrate improved polarization prediction and downstream surface-normal estimation. The dataset and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [9] [The Living Library: Transforming Archival Collections into Conversational Knowledge Systems -- Lessons from the Theodore Roosevelt Presidential Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09368)
*Pengce Wang,Lucia Ronchi Darre,Matt Briney,Michaell Bakalars,Dan Rutkowski,Ursula Hardy,David Wolf,Laura Hoffman,Allen Kim,Shawn Wright,Juan Lavista Ferres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Living Library 框架，将碎片化数字档案转化为可治理的对话式实体展览体验，并在罗斯福总统图书馆部署了名为 Talk to TR 的全尺寸数字人展项，总结了其在公共展览中长期无人值守运行的实践经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字档案通常碎片化且缺乏治理，难以直接支撑面向公众的可信对话体验；同时，在博物馆环境中构建可长时间无人值守、面向真实访客的实体数字人展项，存在事实可靠性、对话流畅性与系统稳定性等多重挑战。

Method: 框架分为四层：数字化与语料构建、AI 处理、检索与推理、以及可选的具身对话界面。前三层汇聚约 30 万条记录，进行 OCR 与结构化元数据增强以供专家策展审核，并发布至稠密/语义混合索引；策展人通过 Archivist App 修正 AI 生成的转录与元数据。核心方法包括 Cross-Era Analogical Grounding（以有据可查的历史类比重构当代问题）、双路径检索与端到端流式响应；系统层面采用分层看门狗、访客会话隔离、自动会话管理与可独立重启的服务保障可靠性。

Result: 系统作为公开展项持续运行，可支持数百名访客的实时面对面交互，并保持响应有据、低延迟与无人值守的稳定运行；同时将头像真实感、空间音频、灯光、舞台布景与会话设计作为整体体验进行开发与评估。

Conclusion: 本文不以受控基准测试为报告目标，而是呈现 Talk to TR 作为公共展项的运营经验，并提出一套可迁移的模型，用于将档案馆藏转化为可信的、面对面的对话式体验。

Abstract: We present the Living Library, an end-to-end framework for transforming fragmented digital archives into governed, conversational, in-person exhibit experiences. Developed and deployed at the Theodore Roosevelt Presidential Library, the framework comprises four layers: digitization and corpus creation, AI-powered processing, retrieval and reasoning, and an optional embodied conversational interface. The first three layers aggregate a 300,000-record collection, apply OCR and structured metadata enrichment for expert curatorial review, and publish records to a hybrid dense/semantic index. Expert review is conducted through the Archivist App, a curator-facing interface that supports correction of AI-generated transcriptions and metadata.
  The governed corpus powers both a researcher-facing interface and Talk to TR, a continuously operating exhibit that embodies Theodore Roosevelt as a full-scale digital human within a museum environment. To support live, face-to-face interactions, Cross-Era Analogical Grounding reframes contemporary questions through documented historical parallels, allowing Roosevelt to address present-day topics without inventing facts. Dual-path retrieval and end-to-end streaming keep responses grounded and responsive. Layered watchdogs, visitor-session isolation, automated conversation management, and independently restartable services enable reliable unattended operation for hundreds of visitors. Avatar realism, spatial audio, lighting, staging, and conversational design are developed and evaluated as an integrated experience. Rather than report a controlled benchmark, we describe lessons from operating Talk to TR as a public exhibit and offer a transferable model for transforming archival collections into believable, in-person conversational experiences.

</details>


### [10] [OmniPoint: Universal Monocular Metric Pointcloud from Any Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09394)
*Botao Ye,Marc Pollefeys,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Abhijit Kundu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniPoint 提出统一框架，通过解耦的射线-距离表示与双向数据增强，实现跨针孔、鱼眼、等距柱状投影的单目度量3D重建，取得零样本SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单目度量3D重建方法受限于固定相机模型假设与僵化的输入方案，难以跨成像传感器泛化，且替代相机训练数据严重稀缺。

Method: 摒弃平面深度回归，采用解耦的射线与距离表示及解耦训练目标，将相机投影模型与场景结构显式分离；引入在3D空间连接有标签透视数据与无标签全向域的双向增强策略；提出鲁棒信息注入机制，用可学习输入状态嵌入消除架构歧义，并以向量化高斯平滑稠密化不规则测量。

Result: 在多个基准上取得state-of-the-art的零样本性能。

Conclusion: OmniPoint 为统一单目3D重建建立了鲁棒的新标准，可泛化于多种成像传感器并灵活兼容可选几何先验输入。

Abstract: Recovering metric 3D geometry from monocular images is a fundamental computer vision task, yet current methods remain heavily fragmented by fixed camera model assumptions and inflexible input schemes. We present OmniPoint, a unified framework designed to generalize metric reconstruction across diverse imaging sensors, including pinhole, fisheye, and equirectangular projections, while accommodating varying geometric priors. To overcome projection rigidity, OmniPoint abandons conventional planar depth regression. It instead adopts a decoupled ray and distance representation alongside a decoupled training objective, explicitly separating the camera projection model from the scene structure. To address the severe scarcity of training data for alternative cameras, we introduce a bidirectional augmentation strategy that explicitly bridges labeled perspective data and unlabeled omnidirectional domains in 3D space. Furthermore, to seamlessly integrate optional inputs like camera intrinsics or sparse depth without destabilizing the network through feature distribution shifts, we propose a robust information injection mechanism. This mechanism utilizes learnable input state embeddings to resolve architectural ambiguity and applies vectorized Gaussian smoothing to densify irregular measurements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniPoint achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance across multiple benchmarks, establishing a robust new standard for unified monocular 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [11] [VANTAGE-Bench: Evaluating the Infrastructure AI Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09396)
*Zaid Pervaiz Bhat,Nimra Nayyar,Arihant Jain,Lap Fung Chan,John Suchanek,Yu Wang,Varun Praveen,Tomasz Kornuta,Vidya Nariyambut Murali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VANTAGE-Bench，用以衡量固定摄像头场景下"基础设施 AI 差距"，覆盖物流、交通、智能空间三域，统一图像与视频的语义、空间、时间、时空四类能力评估，揭示现有 VLM 的不足是"集中性"而非"普遍性"的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身 AI 与 VLM 评测集中于以主体为中心的消费级视频与动作导向任务，忽视了广泛存在的 Physical AI 另一分支——基础设施 AI（依靠固定摄像头进行安全监控与运营记录等开环分析）。这一评测空白导致对 VLM 在真实基础设施场景中的能力缺乏量化认识。

Method: 构建 VANTAGE-Bench：跨 Logistics、Transportation、Smart Spaces 三个运营域；统一图像与视频评测，覆盖语义、空间、时间、时空四类能力；超越多选题，设八种任务形式（含密集描述与时空 grounding）；提出单次轨迹协议用于单目标跟踪，并进行固定摄像头基础设施视频上首个此类评测，与专家跟踪器对比。标注涵盖三种制度，共 3,346 个媒体资产、3,342 条视频任务标注、4,281 条图像 grounding 标注、27,404 个检测框。

Result: 零样本评测 17 个模型：相对消费级基准的短板是集中的而非普遍的——事件验证、指称表达、时间定位在各模型规模上均落后约 9–24 分；而视频问答与 VideoMME 差距在 5.3 分内，2D 空间指点相对 BLINK 无落后。时间维度绝对表现最弱：时间定位无人超过 55.7 mIoU，密集视频描述无人超过 37.3 SODA_c。跟踪上，前沿模型在短时域内与专家跟踪器差距约 5 分，随时域拉长而扩大。开源权重模型在 2D 目标定位上 outright 领先，说明既非规模也非专有访问能解释该模式。

Conclusion: VLM 在基础设施 AI 场景中的能力缺口具有明确的维度集中性，尤其集中于时间语义与精细化定位；模型规模与专有访问并非决定性因素。作者公开数据、评测框架与排行榜，推动对固定摄像头基础设施场景的系统性评测。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) advance toward physical deployment, the focus has remained on action-oriented Embodied AI evaluated on subject-centric consumer video. This overlooks a pervasive class of Physical AI: Infrastructure AI, which relies on fixed cameras for open-loop insights like safety monitoring and operational logging. We introduce VANTAGE-Bench, a benchmark measuring this "Infrastructure AI Gap." It spans three operational domains (Logistics, Transportation, and Smart Spaces), unifies image and video evaluation across semantic, spatial, temporal, and spatio-temporal capabilities, and moves beyond multiple-choice to eight task formulations including dense captioning and spatio-temporal grounding. It adds a single-pass trajectory protocol for Single Object Tracking and, to our knowledge, the first such evaluation on fixed-camera infrastructure video, scored against specialist trackers. Annotation spans three regimes over 3,346 media assets: 3,342 video-task annotations, 4,281 image-grounding annotations, and 27,404 detection boxes.
  Evaluating 17 models zero-shot, we find the shortfall relative to consumer-centric benchmarks is concentrated, not general. Event verification, referring expressions, and temporal localization fall roughly 9 to 24 points at every model scale, while video question answering stays within 5.3 points of VideoMME and 2D spatial pointing shows no shortfall against BLINK. The temporal pillar is weakest in absolute terms: no system exceeds 55.7 mIoU on temporal localization or 37.3 SODA_c on dense video captioning. On tracking, frontier models come within roughly 5 points of specialist trackers over short horizons but separate as the horizon extends. Open-weight models lead 2D object localization outright, so neither scale nor proprietary access explains the pattern. Data, evaluation harness, and leaderboard: https://vantage-bench.org/

</details>


### [12] [Vision-language models know more about agriculture than they show and rubric-grounded verifications close the gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09417)
*Earl Ranario,Jared Smith,Lars Lundqvist,Urmil Jatin Chandarana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了跨116个数据集、834类、8324张图像的农业视觉-语言基准，证明VLM的瓶颈不在视觉表征，而在领域知识调用；提出基于诊断量规的测试时推理与PPT验证器，使F1接近翻倍，但发现验证器置信度分数与正确性负相关，不可用作不确定性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在农业病害、虫害、损伤、品质与物种识别上的零样本性能较差，但尚不清楚这是源于视觉特征薄弱，还是无法将视觉特征与领域知识相连接。作者希望定位性能差距的来源，以便有针对性地改进。

Method: 1) 构建涵盖116个数据集、834类、8324张图像的农业分类基准，覆盖病害、虫害、损伤、品质、物种等任务；2) 用线性探测比较VLM视觉编码器与自监督DINOv3基线的特征可分性；3) 设置oracle参考描述作为参数知识上界、无辅助提示作为下界，进行条件化对比；4) 提出固定任务级诊断量规引导的测试时推理，生成K个候选回答，并由Probabilistic Pivot Tournament（PPT）验证器基于量规两两打分选出最优回答。

Result: 线性探测显示VLM视觉编码器已能编码与DINOv3近乎相当可分的农业特征，排除视觉表征为主要瓶颈；以oracle描述为条件可弥合无辅助下界留下的大部分差距。量规引导的PPT推理使评判F1相对下界近乎翻倍，并在若干任务上达到或超过上界，例如将Gemma 4 E4B-it的病害F1提升至0.71，超过其自身0.60的上界。但验证器的字母量表置信度产生反效果：按最高置信度过滤并不能提升准确率，且在所有模型与候选池规模下与正确性负相关。

Conclusion: VLM在农业领域的主要瓶颈是领域知识调用而非视觉特征；围绕诊断量规的测试时推理能有效释放其潜在知识并大幅提升性能。然而，PPT验证器输出的置信度分数不能作为预测不确定性的度量，且观测到的增益主要来自量规引导的生成而非两两验证环节。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) show promise for agricultural classification, but zero-shot performance on disease, pest, damage, quality, and species identification remains poor, and it is unclear whether this reflects weak visual features or a failure to connect them to domain knowledge. We build a benchmark of 116 datasets, 834 classes, and 8,324 images spanning these tasks to isolate where the gap arises. Linear probing shows VLM vision encoders already encode agricultural features nearly as separable as a self-supervised DINOv3 baseline, ruling out weak visual representations as the primary bottleneck. Conditioning each model on an oracle reference description (an upper bound on its parametric knowledge) closes most of the gap left by an unaided lower bound, showing VLMs already know more about agriculture than they show. To close this gap without an oracle description at inference time, we structure test-time reasoning around a fixed, per-task diagnostic rubric: the model generates $K$ candidate responses and a Probabilistic Pivot Tournament (PPT) verifier, scored pairwise against the rubric, selects the best one. This nearly doubles judged F1 over the lower bound and matches or exceeds the upper bound on several tasks, notably pushing Gemma 4 E4B-it's disease F1 to 0.71, above its own upper bound of 0.60. However, the verifier's letter-scale confidence score has the opposite of its intended effect: filtering to its most confident predictions does not improve accuracy and correlates negatively with correctness across every model and pool size tested, so the score cannot serve as a measure of predictive uncertainty, and most of the observed gain likely comes from rubric-grounded generation rather than pairwise verification.

</details>


### [13] [Longitudinal tracking of multiple sclerosis lesions in the spinal cord: A validation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09424)
*Pierre-Louis Benveniste,Julian McGinnis,Shannon Kolind,Larry D. Lynd,Sarah A. Morrow,Jiwon Oh,Alexandre Prat,Alice Schabas,Penelope Smyth,Roger Tam,Anthony Traboulsee,Mark Mühlau,Herve Lombaert,Julien Cohen-Adad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较五种脊髓多发性硬化（MS）病灶纵向追踪策略，发现基于配准的重叠匹配方法表现最佳，并首次系统分析脊髓病灶实例级对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割方法仅在各随访时间点生成语义化病灶掩膜，缺乏跨时间的实例级一致对应框架，无法刻画病灶出现、消失、分裂或合并等复杂时序模式，因此脊髓MS病灶的纵向表征受限。

Method: 基于多中心队列的纵向MRI数据，比较五种脊髓MS病灶自动追踪策略：分别依赖可变形配准或脊髓解剖参考系统，涵盖基于重叠的匹配、基于坐标的匈牙利算法、梯度提升分类和孪生（Siamese）模型分类。

Result: 采用实例级真阳性、假阳性、假阴性量化追踪精度，并评估一对多与多对一关联；结果显示基于配准的重叠匹配方法性能最佳。

Conclusion: 该研究首次对脊髓病灶实例对应关系进行系统性分析，阐明了基于配准与免配准范式在纵向MS评估中的优势与局限。代码已开源。

Abstract: Longitudinal characterization of multiple sclerosis (MS) lesions remains constrained by the lack of frameworks capable of establishing consistent instance-level correspondences across time. Conventional segmentation approaches produce semantic lesion masks at each visit and therefore fail to capture the complex instance temporal patterns associated with lesion appearance, disappearance, splitting, or merging. This study presents a comparative evaluation of five strategies for automated tracking of spinal cord MS lesions in longitudinal MRI data from a multi-site cohort. The investigated strategies rely either on deformable registration or on a spinal anatomical reference system, and encompass overlap-based matching, coordinate-based Hungarian algorithm, gradient-boosted classification, and Siamese model classification. Tracking accuracy is quantified using instance-level true positives, false positives, and false negatives, allowing to assess the presence of one-to-many and many-to-one associations. Results show best performance for the registration-based overlap method. This study provides the first systematic analysis of lesion-instance correspondence in the spinal cord and outlines the strengths and limitations of registration-based and registration-free paradigms for longitudinal MS assessment. The code is available at http://github.com/ivadomed/longitudinal-sc-ms-lesion-tracking .

</details>


### [14] [Low-Rank Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models with Fixed-Token Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09462)
*Tanvir Muntakim Tonoy,Sajjad Ghiasvand,Mahnoosh Alizadeh,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将 CoOp 的稠密提示矩阵 P 分解为 P=BA，可大幅减少可训练参数；进一步发现 token 侧因子 B 可直接固定，仅训练 embedding 侧因子 A 即可保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoOp 将 CLIP 的提示学习形式化为稠密提示矩阵 P∈R^{m×d}，但每类仅用少量样本训练如此多参数，存在过参数化嫌疑。作者希望探究该矩阵是否冗余，以及能否用更少参数实现同等甚至更好的下游识别与泛化性能。

Method: 将提示矩阵低秩分解为 P=BA，把可训练参数量从 md 降到 r(m+d)；当固定 token 侧因子 B 后进一步降到 rd。在七个 few-shot 基准与两个 CLIP 主干上对比低秩提示与稠密 CoOp，并测试将 B 固定为高斯、正交、SVD 导出乃至随机基，只训练 embedding 侧 A 的效果。此外通过提示因子不对称性与局部更新空间维度差距进行分析，并给出仅需平滑性的收敛性保证。

Result: 低秩提示以远少参数匹配或超越稠密 CoOp，在低样本 base-to-new 泛化上提升最明显；固定 B 为高斯、正交、SVD 或随机基并只训练 A，性能与完全可训练分解相当；源域训练的 B 相对随机 B 无优势。分析表明固定 B 的约束远弱于固定 A，且优化 A 的收敛性可被证明。

Conclusion: 在 CLIP 提示学习场景中，适配能力主要由 embedding 侧系数承载，token 侧基可直接固定，提示矩阵存在显著过参数化。

Abstract: Prompt learning adapts CLIP to downstream recognition by replacing hand-written templates with learned continuous context vectors, which in Context Optimization (CoOp) form a dense prompt matrix $\mathbf{P}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times d}$ trained from only a few examples per class. We study whether this matrix is over-parameterized by factorizing it as $\mathbf{P}=\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}$, which cuts the trainable prompt parameters from $md$ to $r(m+d)$, and to $rd$ once the token-side factor $\mathbf{B}$ is fixed. Across seven few-shot benchmarks and two CLIP backbones, low-rank prompts match or improve dense CoOp at far fewer parameters, with the clearest gains on low-shot base-to-new generalization. We then find that the token-side factor need not be learned at all: fixing $\mathbf{B}$ to a Gaussian, orthogonal, SVD-derived, or even random basis and training only the embedding-side factor $\mathbf{A}$ stays on par with the fully trainable factorization, and a source-trained $\mathbf{B}$ offers no advantage over a random one. A prompt-factor asymmetry and a local update-space dimension gap show why fixing $\mathbf{B}$ is far less restrictive than fixing $\mathbf{A}$, and a smoothness-only guarantee certifies that optimizing $\mathbf{A}$ over a fixed $\mathbf{B}$ converges. In the CLIP prompt setting, the embedding-side coefficients carry the adaptation while the token basis can simply be fixed.

</details>


### [15] [LeCor: Learning to Be Corrected by Meta-Learned Test-Time Training for Interactive 3D Lung-Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09477)
*Yi Luo,Yike Guo,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Rui Zhang,Kai Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LeCor，将交互式分割中的每次修正视为训练信号，对每个病例的轻量适配器进行测试时训练，在肺肿瘤CT分割上显著优于仅靠 SAM 3 记忆条件的微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放疗计划中，CT 肺肿瘤勾画耗时较大，模型生成轮廓需临床医生交互修正。SAM 3 等可提示基础模型通过"会话记忆"写入每次修正来影响未标注切片，但权重固定，导致记忆条件化下未触碰切片的精度在约六轮修正后不再提升，交互收益饱和。

Method: 将每次修正视为训练信号，提出 LeCor：为每个病例维护一小组 case adapter，逐病例重置；通过元学习使由一次点击驱动的单步梯度更新能够改善未被点击的切片，从而在测试时进行适配（test-time training）。

Result: 在来自五个公开CT队列的690例测试集上，微调 SAM 3 后单点提示 Dice 由 0.298 提升至 0.757，七轮修正后达 0.765，但纯记忆条件化下未触碰切片精度六轮后停止改善。在至少跨越八个切片的133例测试集上，LeCor 将七轮修正后的 Dice 由微调模型的 0.787 提升至 0.827，未达 Dice 0.80 的病例数由 47 降至 27，并仅用三轮修正即达到微调模型七轮的精度。

Conclusion: 将交互修正作为测试时训练信号、配合逐病例元学习适配器，可突破固定权重记忆条件化的性能瓶颈，加速交互收敛并提高肺肿瘤CT分割精度。

Abstract: Delineating lung tumours on computed tomography (CT) takes a considerable share of the time spent on radiotherapy planning, and a contour proposed by a model can be refined interactively by the clinician. Promptable foundation models such as SAM 3 support this workflow by writing each correction into a session memory that conditions the remaining slices, while the model weights stay fixed. On 690 test cases from five public CT cohorts, fine-tuning SAM 3 on lung tumours raises the Dice obtained from a single point prompt from 0.298 to 0.757, and seven rounds of corrections raise it further to 0.765, but under memory conditioning alone the accuracy on slices the annotator has not touched stops improving after six rounds. We therefore treat each correction as a training signal and propose LeCor, which performs test-time training on a small set of case adapters that are reset for every case and meta-learned such that a single gradient step driven by a click improves the slices that were not clicked. On the 133 test cases that span at least eight slices, LeCor raises the Dice reached after seven correction rounds from 0.787 with the fine-tuned model to 0.827, reduces the number of cases that never reach a Dice of 0.80 from 47 to 27, and reaches in three correction rounds the accuracy that the fine-tuned model attains in seven.

</details>


### [16] [Infra-Bench CLS: A Global, Open-Source Benchmark for Critical Infrastructure Classification with Earth Observation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09482)
*Justin Guthrie,Edward Oughton,Konrad Wessels,Matthew Rice,Isaac Corley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Infra-Bench CLS 基准，用 Sentinel-1/2 的设施级关键基础设施图像评测地球观测基础模型，结果显示基础模型显著优于监督基线，但电力部门类别表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施位置数据全球分布不均、尤其发展中地区缺失。地球观测基础模型被寄予厚望，但其在设施级关键基础设施检测与分类这一挑战性下游任务上的有效性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 构建 Infra-Bench CLS 基准，包含 18,756 幅 Sentinel-1 SAR 与 Sentinel-2 多光谱设施级图像，覆盖七大洲、13 个基础设施类别（报告其中保留的 10 类）。采用线性探测与微调两种方式，在 1.0x 与 0.3x 两个训练数据规模下评测 7 个基础模型（SatlasPretrain S2、SatlasPretrain S1、CROMA、Prithvi-EO-2.0、AlphaEarth Foundations、OlmoEarth v1.1-Base、DINOv3 ViT-L/16）。

Result: 与 ResNet-18 监督基线 macro F1 的 39.2% 相比，最佳基础模型达 57.9%，提升 48%。表现最好的类别为机场（F1 85.3%）、火车站（82.1%）和数据中心（77.6%）；电力部门多数类别表现不佳（F1 27.5–46.2%）。

Conclusion: 基础模型能够实现更优的关键基础设施分类，但未来需评估更高分辨率影像上的表现，尤其针对电力等性能较差的部门。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure location data is often incomplete and unevenly distributed globally, especially in developing regions. Earth observation foundation models are proposed as a new step in enabling us to more efficiently understand the natural and built environment, raising questions as to their effectiveness in performing challenging downstream tasks. Yet, foundation models remain largely untested for detecting and classifying the facility-scale critical infrastructure that underpins a range of important societal and economic functions. Subsequently, Infra-Bench CLS is introduced as a benchmark to test foundation models on 18,756 Sentinel-1 SAR and Sentinel-2 multispectral facility-scale critical infrastructure asset images covering seven continents and 13 infrastructure classes, with results reported for the 10 retained classes. Using linear probing and fine-tuning for two training dataset levels (1.0x and 0.3x), seven foundation models are evaluated (SatlasPretrain S2, SatlasPretrain S1, CROMA, Prithvi-EO-2.0, AlphaEarth Foundations, OlmoEarth v1.1-Base, and DINOv3 ViT-L/16). When comparing macro F1 scores to a ResNet-18 supervised baseline of 39.2 percent, the best foundation model achieved 57.9 percent, a 48 percent improvement. Top performing classes were airports (F1 85.3 percent), train stations (F1 82.1 percent), and data centers (F1 77.6 percent). By contrast, many of the power sector classes perform poorly (F1 27.5-46.2 percent). These findings suggest foundation models can enable superior critical infrastructure classification, but future work should evaluate performance on higher-resolution imagery, particularly for poorly performing sectors, such as power.

</details>


### [17] [Learning Global Camera Poses from Noisy View-Graphs for Structure from Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09491)
*Fadi Khatib,Meirav Galun,Ronen Basri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于可学习视图图聚合的全局SfM框架，用置换等变、边条件图神经网络从含噪相对位姿回归全局一致的相机外参，无需真值监督，配合三角化与光束法平差，在多个数据集上兼具精度、可扩展性与速度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机位姿估计是三维重建与视图合成流程的关键环节。现有深度方法多依赖真值监督、面向轨迹中心（track-centric），在大规模场景、图密度变化下效率与鲁棒性不足；经典全局SfM流程精度高但速度慢。因此需要一种高效、可扩展且对图密度鲁棒的全局SfM方案。

Method: 提出深度全局Structure-from-Motion框架，核心为可学习的视图图聚合：采用置换等变（permutation-equivariant）、边条件（edge-conditioned）的图神经网络，输入为含噪的成对相对位姿，输出全局一致的相机外参。训练不依赖真值，仅使用相对位姿一致性目标（relative-pose consistency objective）。网络输出后接三维点三角化与鲁棒光束法平差（bundle adjustment）。

Result: 在MegaDepth、1DSfM、Strecha与BlendedMVS四个数据集上评估。实验表明：相比以轨迹为中心的深度方法，本方法在旋转与平移精度上更优，并在多场景中配准更多图像；与最先进经典流程相比取得有竞争力的结果，同时速度显著更快。方法高效，可扩展至千张以上图像，且对图密度鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该工作以图神经网络聚合视图图实现全局相机位姿回归，摆脱真值监督，兼顾大规模扩展性、图密度鲁棒性与运行效率，在精度上超越深度轨迹中心方法、并可与经典SOTA流程竞争而更快，为深度全局SfM提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Camera pose estimation is a key step in 3D reconstruction and view-synthesis pipelines. We present a deep, global Structure-from-Motion framework based on learned view-graph aggregation. Our method employs a permutation-equivariant, edge-conditioned graph neural network that takes noisy pairwise relative poses as input and outputs globally consistent camera extrinsics. The network is trained without ground-truth supervision, relying solely on a relative-pose consistency objective. This is followed by 3D point triangulation and robust bundle adjustment. Our approach is efficient, scalable to more than a thousand images, and robust to graph density. We evaluate our method on MegaDepth, 1DSfM, Strecha, and BlendedMVS. These experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior rotation and translation accuracy compared to deep track-centric methods while registering more images across many scenes, and competitive results compared to state-of-the-art classical pipelines, while being much faster.

</details>


### [18] [RoMa-$Ω$: What Feed-Forward 3D Models Know About Image Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09507)
*David Nordström,Xinyue Zhang,Thibaut Loiseau,Vincent Lepetit,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究前馈三维重建模型（如VGGT）对图像匹配的表征能力，发现其零样本匹配较弱但线性探测与全流程匹配潜力强，并据此用VGGT-Ω替换RoMa v2的DINO主干，取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式图像匹配（如RoMa）与前馈重建模型（如VGGT）界限日益模糊，尤其MASt3R、VGGT-Ω引入匹配损失后。因此需回答一个核心问题：前馈三维模型究竟"懂"多少图像匹配？

Method: 从三个维度系统分析：(i) patch特征的零样本匹配；(ii) 三维点预测的直接匹配；(iii) 在所学表征上训练完整匹配器。并据此将RoMa v2的DINO主干替换为VGGT-Ω，重新训练得到新模型。

Result: 前馈重建模型零样本匹配表现较差（尤其在深层），但为线性探测和完整匹配流程提供强表征；无训练情况下其原始预测在中等视角变化与模态差异下即可实现有竞争力的匹配。重训后的模型在多个基准上超越SOTA匹配器，如在WxBS上较RoMa v2提升+8.1 mAA。

Conclusion: 前馈三维重建模型的表征对匹配任务高度有效，可作为匹配器主干替代传统DINO特征，显著提升匹配性能，揭示了重建与匹配任务的深层联系。

Abstract: Learned image matching has experienced significant progress in recent years, culminating in robust and accurate matchers such as RoMa, whose robustness is often attributed to its use of frozen DINO features. In a parallel development, feed-forward reconstruction models, such as VGGT, have been trained on ever-growing datasets to accurately regress dense 3D point maps and camera poses. The distinction between matchers and feed-forward reconstruction models has become increasingly blurred with the introduction of matching losses in models such as MASt3R and VGGT-$Ω$. This raises a natural question: what do feed-forward 3D models know about image matching? In this work, we answer this question by analyzing three scenarios: (i) zero-shot matching of patch features, (ii) direct matching of 3D point predictions, and (iii) training a full matcher on top of the learned representations. We find that, despite performing poorly in zero-shot matching, especially in later layers, feed-forward reconstruction models provide strong representations for linear probing and full matching pipelines. We further show that, even without any training, their raw predictions alone enable competitive matching, albeit only under moderate viewpoint changes and modality gaps. Based on these insights, we retrain RoMa v2 by replacing its DINO backbone with VGGT-$Ω$. Our resulting model, \ours, outperforms state-of-the-art matchers on a wide range of benchmarks, e.g. +8.1 mAA compared to RoMa v2 on WxBS.

</details>


### [19] [AnimalLift: Reconstructing Animatable 3D Animals from a Single Image by Learning Canonical Shape, Texture, and Fur Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09513)
*Chunyi Sun,Ruyi Zha,Weijian Deng,Junlin Han,Dylan Campbell,Stephen Gould*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnimalLift：从单张图像重建具有显式毛发、统一拓扑与UV参数化的可动画3D动物资产，支持动画、姿态迁移、毛发编辑与仿真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像重建完全可动画的3D动物仍具挑战：动画级资产不仅需要合理几何，还需统一拓扑、可编辑外观以及与形变、仿真兼容的毛发表示。现有图像到3D方法多依赖隐式或松散结构表示，难以绑定骨骼或编辑；而参数化动物模型虽支持动画，却无法刻画细节纹理与毛发外观。

Method: 提出AnimalLift框架：将输入图像提升到具有跨数据集一致拓扑与UV参数化的共享规范空间，在统一前馈架构中联合预测规范几何、纹理与毛发。核心表示为UV对齐的毛发贴图（UV-aligned fur map），在表面对齐的规范域中编码毛发几何，实现与网格形变及毛发仿真兼容的显式毛发重建。同时提出程序化数据生成流水线，为多物种、多外观提供几何、纹理、毛发对齐的大规模监督。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，方法具有较高的重建质量，并在不同动物类别间展现良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化表示除重建外，可直接支持动画、姿态迁移、毛发编辑及仿真兼容渲染等下游应用。

Abstract: Reconstructing a fully animatable 3D animal from a single image remains challenging because animation-ready assets require not only plausible geometry, but also a unified topology, editable appearance, and fur representations compatible with deformation and simulation. Existing image-to-3D approaches often rely on implicit or loosely structured representations that are difficult to rig or edit, while parametric animal models support animation but cannot capture detailed texture and fur appearance. We present AnimalLift, a framework for reconstructing structured, animation-compatible 3D animal assets with explicit fur from a single image. Our method lifts an input image into a shared canonical space with a consistent topology and UV parameterization across the dataset, enabling joint prediction of canonical geometry, texture, and fur in a unified feed-forward architecture. A key component of our representation is a UV-aligned fur map that encodes strand geometry in a surface-aligned canonical domain, allowing explicit fur reconstruction compatible with mesh deformation and fur simulation. To train the model, we introduce a procedural data generation pipeline that provides large-scale supervision with aligned geometry, texture, and fur across diverse animal species and appearances. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate strong reconstruction quality and generalization across animal categories. Beyond reconstruction, our structured representation directly supports downstream applications including animation, pose transfer, fur editing, and simulation-compatible rendering.

</details>


### [20] [MotionBlind: Probing the Illusion of Motion Understanding in Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09528)
*Dhairya Bhatia,Bishoy Galoaa,Oliver Fritsche,Shahid Kamal,Muhammad Obaidullah Abdul Salam,Umer Saleem,Om Rastogi,Frania Felix Chettiar,Nesli Erdogmus,Sarah Ostadabbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MotionBlind 基准，用自录视频对比对测试 Video-LLM 的物理运动感知，发现绝大多数模型无法辨别速度、幅度与方向，仅 Gemini3.1 Pro 整体通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Video-LLM 被日益用作世界模型的感知前端，这一角色预设其能“读懂运动”（多快、朝哪、被推得多重）。作者要验证这一预设是否成立，并指出若前端无法区分同一动作的不同速度，就不能作为世界模型可靠的监督、奖励或评测来源。

Method: 提出 MotionBlind，一个基于自录视频的对比基准，聚焦物理运动（速度、幅度、方向）。每个实例为一对近乎相同、仅运动不同的视频片段；每段配两个互补的是/否问题，每实例共四题，需全部答对才计分，指标为 Instance Accuracy（IAcc，随机下限 6.25%）。对六个开源与两个前沿 Video-LLM 做受控实验，变量包括视频是否存在、帧序是否正确、帧采样数（1–24 帧）与四种选帧策略。单帧、外观、纯语言捷径均会退化至下限。

Result: 开源模型 IAcc 接近 6.25% 下限，扩大模型规模无帮助。移除视频后所有模型 IAcc 降为 0，打乱帧序后 IAcc 跌至随机水平，说明任务确需有序视频。增加帧数或改进选帧策略均无法弥补差距。仅 Gemini3.1 Pro 整体通过该基准，但在速度维度上仍然失败。

Conclusion: Video-LLM 目前缺乏物理运动感知能力，且该缺陷不能靠更多帧或更优帧选择解决，因为二者只改变“看到哪些帧”，而非“是否读取了运动”。MotionBlind 与 TimeBlind 互为补充。作者认为无法区分同一动作两种速度的感知前端，尚不足以作为世界模型的可靠监督来源。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) are increasingly used as the perceptual front end of world models, a role that assumes they can read motion: how fast something moves, which way it travels, how hard it is pushed. We show they cannot. A Video-LLM can watch two clips of the same person in the same room, name every object in both, and still fail to say which clip moves faster. We introduce MotionBlind, a contrastive benchmark of self-recorded video for physically grounded motion(speed, magnitude, and direction), the variables a world model must predict. Each instance is a pair of near-identical clips that differ only in motion. Each clip carries two complementary yes/no questions, giving four items per instance, and a model earns credit only if all four are correct. We report Instance Accuracy(IAcc), which has a 6.25% chance floor. Single-frame, appearance, and language-only shortcuts all collapse to it. MotionBlind complements the recent TimeBlind benchmark. We run a controlled study of six open and two frontier Video-LLMs, varying whether the video is present, whether frames are shown in the correct temporal order, and how frames are sampled (1 to 24 frames, four selection strategies). Open models sit near the 6.25% floor, and scale does not help. Removing the video drops every model to zero IAcc, and shuffling frames collapses IAcc to chance, so the task genuinely needs video in order. Neither more frames nor smarter frame selection closes the gap, because these change which frames are seen, not whether motion is read. Only Gemini3.1 Pro clears the benchmark overall, and even it fails on speed. A frontend that cannot tell two speeds of the same action apart is not yet a trustworthy source of supervision, reward, or evaluation for a world model.

</details>


### [21] [RouteBridge: Reliability-Routed Bidirectional Distillation Between Neural Radiance Fields and 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09606)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RouteBridge，一种逐光线双向选择教学方向、并可在不可靠时弃权的 NeRF 与 3DGS 跨表示蒸馏框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF 与 3DGS 的归纳偏置互补，但现有跨表示蒸馏通常全程固定某一表示为教师，导致局部重建误差被传播。

Method: 可靠性估计器结合光度残差与表示特有的几何证据，为每条光线选择 NeRF→3DGS、3DGS→NeRF 或弃权；并通过与渲染器无关的接口传递颜色、不透明度与归一化深度，无需共享特征或点对应。

Result: 在 mip-NeRF 360 上，NeRF 与 3DGS 导出分别为 28.56 dB 和 28.77 dB；3DGS 导出较 3DGS 提升 1.56 dB、较 NeRF-GS 提升 0.45 dB，LPIPS 降至 0.207。静态三视图 DTU 上为 21.12 dB。消融表明自适应路由与几何光线目标均有贡献。

Conclusion: 逐光线的自适应可弃权路由能有效缓解固定教师带来的误差传播，双向跨表示蒸馏同时提升 NeRF 与 3DGS 的渲染质量。

Abstract: Neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) encode a scene with complementary inductive biases, but existing cross-representation distillation typically fixes one representation as teacher for the entire scene. A globally fixed teacher can propagate local reconstruction errors. We present RouteBridge, a bidirectional framework that selects the teaching direction for each ray. Its reliability estimator combines photometric residuals with representation-specific geometric evidence and routes supervision from NeRF to 3DGS, from 3DGS to NeRF, or abstains. A renderer-independent interface transfers color, opacity, and normalized depth without shared features or point correspondence. On mip-NeRF 360, the NeRF and 3DGS exports reach 28.56 and 28.77 dB, respectively. The 3DGS export improves over 3DGS by 1.56 dB and over NeRF-GS by 0.45 dB while reducing LPIPS to 0.207. On static three-view DTU, RouteBridge obtains 21.12 dB. Ablations show that both adaptive routing and geometric ray targets contribute to the improvement.

</details>


### [22] [Marker-free eye-gaze estimation using a single image and depth from defocus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09610)
*David Hurtubise-Martin,Feriel Fass,Djemel Ziou,Marie-Flavie Auclair-Fortier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅用单个2D摄像头（如笔记本内置摄像头）的无标记视线估计方法，通过虹膜定位与基于散焦深度的头部姿态估计提取视线特征，并用变分贝叶斯多项逻辑回归建立特征到注视点的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视线估计多依赖外部标记或专用硬件，限制了自然场景下的应用。作者希望实现无需标记、仅用普通2D摄像头即可完成的视线估计，以提高实用性与部署便利性。

Method: 从虹膜定位结果与基于散焦深度（depth from defocus）估计的头部姿态中提取视线相关特征，构成8维特征向量（头部姿态与虹膜位移参数）；采用变分贝叶斯多项逻辑回归（variational Bayesian multinomial logistic regression）作为从特征到注视点位置的映射模型；全程不使用任何外部标记。

Result: 在不同观看距离下，针对观看计算机屏幕的被试进行视线估计实验，并与五种现有方法进行对比；所得评分表明所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提无标记、单2D摄像头的视线估计方案在对比实验中表现有效，验证了将虹膜位移与散焦深度头部姿态结合、并用变分贝叶斯多项逻辑回归映射的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a marker-free eye-gaze estimation approach using a single 2D camera, such as an integrated laptop webcam. The gaze-related features are estimated from iris localization and head pose estimated by using depth from defocus. A variational Bayesian multinomial logistic regression framework is used as mapping from the estimated features to the position of regard, based on an 8-dimensional feature vector of head-pose and iris-displacement parameters. No external marker is needed. Experiments were conducted by estimating the gaze of people watching a computer screen at different distances and compared against five existing methods. The obtained scores demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [23] [Hyperbolic Geometry for Open-World Object Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09626)
*Wuzhou Li,Jiawei Zhou,Shenghang Wang,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出HyRS-OWOD，将双曲几何引入遥感开放世界目标检测，通过DOL、HUL和HML三个模块提升未知目标召回与增量学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感开放世界目标检测（OWOD）要求模型识别未知目标并在标注可用后增量学习。遥感目标类别常具有潜在层级关系，而现有方法多采用欧氏空间，难以充分表达此类层级结构，从而限制了未知目标召回与增量学习效果。

Method: 提出HyRS-OWOD，基于双曲几何建模。未知目标发现采用两步机制：解耦目标性学习（DOL）将前景感知与语义信息解耦，从背景中分离前景候选；双曲不确定性学习（HUL）利用双曲嵌入的半径作为不确定性线索，区分已知与未知。增量学习方面提出双曲度量学习（HML），增强类间可分性，促进新类融入并缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在三个遥感基准数据集上的实验表明，相比当前最先进的OWOD方法，该方法在未知目标召回与增量学习方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 双曲几何能有效刻画遥感目标的层级结构，HyRS-OWOD验证了其在开放世界检测中提升未知召回与增量学习性能的有效性。

Abstract: Open-world object detection (OWOD) extends closed-set detection by requiring models to identify unknown objects and incrementally learn them once annotations become available. In remote sensing imagery, object categories often exhibit latent hierarchical relationships that may be inadequately represented in the Euclidean spaces commonly adopted by existing methods, limiting unknown-object recall and incremental-learning performance. To address this issue, we investigate hyperbolic geometry for OWOD in remote sensing imagery and propose HyRS-OWOD. To improve unknown object recall, we design a two-step unknown-object discovery mechanism: a Decoupled Objectness Learning (DOL) module that disentangles foreground perception from semantic information to separate foreground proposals from background regions, followed by a Hyperbolic Uncertainty Learning (HUL) component that leverages the radius of hyperbolic embeddings as an uncertainty-aware cue for known-unknown discrimination. For incremental learning, we develop a Hyperbolic Metric Learning (HML) strategy that enhances inter-class separability, facilitating the incorporation of novel categories while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Experiments on three remote sensing benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in unknown recall and incremental learning over state-of-the-art OWOD methods.

</details>


### [24] [LightMedSeg-ISLES: Stroke Lesion Segmentation with 81x Fewer Parameters than nnU-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09634)
*Giorgi Nikvashvili,Hanxue Gu,Jie Bao,Kang Wang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightMedSeg-ISLES以126万参数实现接近亿级nnU-Net的卒中病灶分割性能，是高效轻量的单检查点替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型网络虽在医学图像分割中表现出色，但存储与推理开销大，难以部署；需要一种高性价比的轻量方案在ISLES'26 T1加权卒中病灶分割任务上兼顾性能与效率。

Method: 提出LightMedSeg-ISLES流程（126万参数），在ISLES'26任务上采用翻转测试时增强（flip TTA，四遍）与尺寸过滤；同时以102.35M参数的nnU-Net ResEnc-L作为强基线，并对比UNETR++、nnFormer；通过延长训练与增强增强在不增加容量的情况下提升精度。

Result: 146例留出队列上，LightMedSeg翻转TTA取得0.618平均Dice与0.599病灶级F1；nnU-Net ResEnc-L经尺寸过滤后为0.634 Dice与0.544病灶级F1。方法保留nnU-Net Dice的97.5%，参数少81.4倍，病灶级F1提升0.055，四遍TTA每标准化patch的FLOPs少4.7倍，并略优于过滤后的UNETR++与nnFormer；延长训练与更强增强额外带来0.0358 Dice增益。

Conclusion: 在极低参数量下单检查点即可逼近大模型分割性能，并在病灶级F1与计算效率上更具优势，为部署受限场景提供了强轻量替代方案。

Abstract: Large networks and ensembles often lead medical image segmentation challenges, but their storage and inference demands complicate deployment. We present LightMedSeg-ISLES, a 1.26-million-parameter pipeline for T1-weighted stroke lesion segmentation in ISLES'26. On a 146-case held-out cohort, flip test-time augmentation produces 0.618 mean Dice and 0.599 lesion-wise F1. A 102.35-million-parameter nnU-Net ResEnc-L produces 0.634 Dice and 0.544 lesion-wise F1 after size filtering. LightMedSeg therefore retains 97.5\% of nnU-Net's Dice with 81.4$\times$ fewer parameters while improving lesion-wise F1 by 0.055. Its four-pass TTA operating point requires 4.7$\times$ fewer FLOPs per standardized patch than nnU-Net. It also slightly exceeds filtered UNETR++ and nnFormer. Longer training and stronger augmentation add 0.0358 Dice without increasing capacity, establishing a strong single-checkpoint alternative to much larger models.

</details>


### [25] [Recovering Biomechanical Signals from Missing Keypoints Using Temporal Interpolation in Monocular Gait Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09670)
*Shubham Jariwala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在单目步态分析中，缺失踝关节关键点会使膝角误差升至23.4°且方差趋近于零，而简单的一阶时间插值可将误差降至1.1°并恢复信号方差与平滑度，说明无需复杂学习模型即可恢复缺失关节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目姿态估计可实现低成本步态分析，但对遮挡、检测错误或效率驱动的模型简化所导致的关键点缺失十分敏感。已有恢复缺失关节的工作多聚焦于复杂学习模型，而简单时间方法的有效性尚未被充分探究，因此本文评估在踝关节缺失条件下膝角估计的表现。

Method: 在527帧单目步行视频（其中428帧具有有效基线检测）上，构造踝关节关键点缺失条件，并以一阶时间插值方案作为恢复机制，评估膝角估计的误差、信号方差与平滑度。

Result: 移除踝关节关键点后，平均角度误差增至23.4° ± 46.7°，信号方差崩溃至接近零；时间插值将误差降至1.1° ± 6.7°，并使方差与平滑度恢复到基线几个百分点以内的水平。

Conclusion: 步态信号具有充分的时间冗余性，使计算上极为简单的一阶时间插值即可恢复关键缺失关节，无需依赖学习式重建模型；该发现支持在资源受限或易遮挡的单目场景中采用低复杂度、实时兼容的步态分析设计。

Abstract: Monocular pose estimation enables low-cost gait analysis but is sensitive to missing keypoints caused by occlusion, detection errors, or efficiency-driven model reduction. While prior work on recovering missing joints focuses on complex learned models, the effectiveness of simple temporal methods remains underexplored. We evaluate knee-angle estimation under a missing-ankle-keypoint condition and test a first-order temporal interpolation scheme as a recovery mechanism. Across 527 frames of monocular walking video (428 with valid baseline detections), removing the ankle keypoint increased mean angular error to 23.4° +/- 46.7° and collapsed signal variance to near zero. Temporal interpolation reduced error to 1.1° +/- 6.7° and restored variance and smoothness to within a few percent of baseline. These results indicate that gait signals possess sufficient temporal redundancy for a simple, computationally trivial interpolation scheme to recover a critical missing joint, without resorting to learned reconstruction models. The findings support low-complexity, real-time-compatible designs for gait analysis in resource-constrained or occlusion-prone monocular settings.

</details>


### [26] [Cross-Species Animal Re-Identification with Semantic Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09705)
*Shuoyi Chen,Yuejia Li,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义一致性学习（SCL）框架，通过FDSNorm与CNM两个模块解决跨物种动物ReID中特征空间碎片化问题，在11个公开数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用动物重识别需跨物种识别形态与生态背景各异的个体。与行人ReID不同，动物物种间解剖结构和视觉模式差异巨大，难以建立共享的视觉对应关系，导致跨物种表示碎片化，严重限制泛化能力。

Method: SCL包含两个互补模块：前景-背景解耦光谱归一化（FDSNorm）以区域感知方式抑制环境引起的风格变化，稳定特征统计量；跨物种邻域建模（CNM）通过动态特征邻域捕获跨物种可迁移的关系结构。

Result: 在11个公开动物ReID数据集、多种跨物种评估协议下持续优于现有最先进方法，并能有效泛化到未见过的物种与生态域。

Conclusion: SCL通过学习跨外观变化稳定且保留跨物种共享语义结构的表示，有效缓解嵌入空间碎片化，提升跨物种泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Generalizable animal Re-Identification (ReID) aims to recognize individual animals across species with diverse morphologies and ecological contexts. Unlike person ReID, where different domains share similar body structures, animal species often exhibit drastically different anatomical structures and visual patterns, making it difficult to establish shared visual correspondences. As a result, representations learned across species tend to form fragmented embedding spaces, which severely limits cross-species generalization. To address this challenge, we propose Semantic Consistency Learning (SCL), a framework designed to learn representations that remain stable across appearance variations while preserving semantic structures shared across species. SCL consists of two complementary components. Foreground-Background Decoupled Spectral Normalization (FDSNorm) stabilizes feature statistics by suppressing environment-induced style variations in a region-aware manner, while Cross-species Neighborhood Modeling (CNM) captures transferable relational structures across species through dynamic feature neighborhoods. Extensive experiments on 11 public animal ReID datasets demonstrate that SCL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under multiple cross-species evaluation protocols and generalizes effectively to previously unseen species and ecological domains. Code is available at https://github.com/Kemalau/ECCV-26-SCL.

</details>


### [27] [VFNet: Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Model for Void Fraction Estimation in Gas-Liquid Two-Phase Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09711)
*Md Adnan Faisal Hossain,Raghav Rajeev,Kumar Nishant,Justin A Weibel,Satish Kumar,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VFNet双分支时空神经网络，从同步多视角视频预测气液两相流空泡率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空泡率是气液两相流表征的关键参数。现有估计方法要么依赖难以跨流体泛化的流动假设，要么依赖扰动流场的侵入式传感。

Method: VFNet采用双分支结构：局部分支从受限空间区域提取特征并融合同步双视角信息；时空分支捕捉流场在空间与时间上的全局演化，用以优化粗略的几何估计。模型在具有已知空泡率真值的CFD仿真数据上训练。

Result: 在广泛的评价指标上，VFNet均优于学习型与传统基线方法；在真实两相流数据上，VFNet还提升了后续的流型分类性能。

Conclusion: 多视角视频结合双分支时空网络可在无需侵入式传感和特定流动假设的前提下实现高精度空泡率预测，并有利于下游流型识别任务。

Abstract: Void fraction, which quantifies the proportion of the fluid flow volume occupied by the gas phase, is a key parameter in the characterization of gas-liquid two-phase flow. Existing estimation methods either rely on flow assumptions that do not generalize across different fluids or on intrusive sensing that disturbs the flow behavior. We propose VFNet, a dual-branch spatio-temporal neural network for void-fraction prediction from synchronized multi-view videos of two-phase flow. A local branch extracts features from confined spatial regions and fuses the synchronized dual views, while a spatio-temporal branch captures the global evolution of the flow across space and time to refine a coarse geometric estimate. Trained on simulated computational fluid dynamics (CFD) data with known ground-truth void fractions and evaluated against both learning-based and traditional baselines, VFNet achieves the best performance across a broad range of metrics and also improves downstream flow-pattern classification on real two-phase flow data.

</details>


### [28] [IAE-VTG: Interaction-Aligned Action-Entity Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09736)
*Shiwen Zhao,Qi Zhang,Sezer Karaoglu,Theo Gevers,Martin R. Oswald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IAE-VTG，从表征与训练分配两个层面建模"动作-实体"交互关系，提升视频时序定位的语义准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTG方法将查询整体编码或仅做通用视频-文本交互，未显式判断动作与实体是否共同出现，易选中"包含两个概念但不构成所描述事件"的片段。此外，二分匹配的训练目标仅依赖时间重叠，会引入时间上合理但语义错误的监督信号。

Method: IAE-VTG包含两个组件：1）细粒度解耦交互模块（FDIM），将查询中动作与实体相关信息分离，分别与互补的运动特征和外观特征对齐，再通过token级交互融合，构建能刻画动作-实体关系的表征；2）交互敏感分配（ISA），将交互证据加入二分匹配，使训练目标同时依据时间重叠与语义兼容性进行选择。

Result: 在QVHighlights、Charades-STA、TACoS上，IAE-VTG一致优于强基线，在标准定位指标上达到有竞争力或SOTA性能。分析表明，当相似动作或实体在多个时间点出现时效果尤为显著，并为复杂事件生成更可靠的分配。

Conclusion: 显式建模动作-实体交互关系，并在表征与训练分配两个层面加以利用，可有效缓解语义误匹配问题，提升VTG的定位可靠性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) localizes the video segment that matches a natural-language query. Many queries describe an action performed by a particular entity. Existing methods often encode the query as a whole or use general video-text interactions, without explicitly checking whether the action and entity occur together. They may therefore select a segment that contains both concepts but not the event described by the query. We propose Interaction Aligned Action-Entity Video Temporal Grounding (IAE-VTG), which models this rela?tionship at both the representation and training assignment levels. First, the Fine-grained Disentangled Interaction Module (FDIM) separates action and entity related query information and aligns it with complementary motion and appearance features. It then combines token-level interactions to build representations that capture the relationship between the action and entity. Second, Interaction-Sensitive Assignment (ISA) adds this interaction evidence to bipartite matching, so training targets are selected using both temporal overlap and semantic compatibility. This reduces supervision from temporally plausible but semantically incorrect proposals. Experiments on QVHighlights, Charades?STA, and TACoS show that IAE-VTG consistently improves strong baselines and achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance on standard grounding metrics. Additional analyses show that the method is especially effective when similar actions or entities appear at multiple times and produces more reliable assignments for complex events.

</details>


### [29] [Distilling Image Prototypes for Guided Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09737)
*Liwen Wang,Xingbo Dong,Iman Yi Liao,Deyin Liu,Massimo Tistarelli,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DIPTTA框架，通过蒸馏图像原型（DIP）作为可再生的源知识锚点，实现动态特征回放与源校准不确定性估计，同时缓解灾难性遗忘和误差累积，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应（TTA）面临两大挑战：噪声伪标签导致的误差累积，以及源知识的灾难性遗忘。现有不确定性方法往往高置信或计算昂贵，而基于原型回放的防遗忘策略依赖静态表征，随模型自适应易失配。因此需要一个动态、可靠且低成本的知识锚定机制。

Method: 提出DIPTTA框架，核心是蒸馏图像原型（DIP）——一组紧凑的合成图像，作为源知识的动态可再生锚点。基于DIP设计动态特征回放机制，持续生成与当前模型状态对齐的特征原型以防止遗忘；同时以DIP锚定源校准的不确定性估计方法，借助稳定的源知识提供偏差更小的样本可靠性度量，从而抑制误差累积。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，DIPTTA显著优于现有最先进方法，尤其在严重域偏移场景下表现突出。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以合成图像原型作为动态源知识锚点，可同时有效应对TTA中的误差累积与灾难性遗忘问题，为测试时自适应提供了一种兼顾动态性与稳定性的新思路。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) enhances the robustness of models against distribution shifts but faces two critical challenges: error accumulation from noisy pseudo-labels and catastrophic forgetting of source knowledge. Uncertainty-based approaches designed to mitigate error accumulation often yield overconfident or computationally expensive estimates, while strategies intended to prevent forgetting via prototype replay rely on static representations that easily become misaligned as the model adapts. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel framework, Distilling Image Prototype for Guided Test-Time Adaptation (DIPTTA). The core of the proposed approach is the introduction of a Distill Image Prototype (DIP), a compact set of synthetic images that serves as a dynamic and regenerative anchor of source knowledge. This prototype enables a dynamic feature replay mechanism that continuously generates feature prototypes aligned with the current state of the model, thus effectively preventing catastrophic forgetting. Furthermore, the DIP anchors a source-calibrated uncertainty estimation method, which provides a less biased measure of sample reliability by leveraging stable source knowledge, thereby robustly suppressing error accumulation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that DIPTTA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly under severe domain shifts. The source code is available at https://github.com/LiwenWang919/DIPTTA.

</details>


### [30] [MethaneFuse: Learning from Multi-Sensor Satellite Observations for Methane Plume Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09762)
*Yuyao Wang,Juliana Y. Leung,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出时序多传感器数据集MethaneUnion与检测模型MethaneFuse，在传感器观测不完整条件下实现甲烷羽流检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星甲烷羽流检测受限于观测不完整：公开卫星在空间、光谱与大气证据上互补，但受重访周期、云覆盖、采集质量及排放瞬变性影响，真实羽流案例极少具备完整的多传感器配对测量。多数基于学习的方法依赖单一传感器输入（尤其是Sentinel-2），导致大量已报告羽流案例无法利用。

Method: 基于Carbon Mapper羽流报告构建时序多传感器数据集MethaneUnion，匹配S2、Landsat 8/9、EMIT与Sentinel-5P观测；在此基础上提出MethaneFuse，在部分传感器可用（无需四传感器完整测量）条件下从异构卫星观测中学习。

Result: 可用覆盖从3,211个有效S2匹配羽流案例扩展至8,981个具备多传感器观测的报告案例。在代表性480 m设置下，MethaneFuse取得84.87 F1与93.62 AUROC，较最强基线提升5.65 F1与8.30 AUROC，假阳性降低8.19个百分点。传感器可用性实验表明：S2可用时检测性能提升，S2不可用时可将羽流知识迁移至L8/9、EMIT与S5P。

Conclusion: 结果证明利用不完整的异构传感器观测进行学习，对实用化甲烷羽流检测具有重要价值。

Abstract: Methane plume detection from satellite imagery is constrained by incomplete observations: public satellites provide complementary spatial, spectral, and atmospheric evidence, but real plume cases rarely contain fully paired multi-sensor measurements because of revisit schedules, cloud coverage, acquisition quality, and the transient nature of emissions. Most learning-based detectors rely on single-sensor inputs, especially Sentinel-2 (S2), leaving many reported plume cases unusable.
  We construct MethaneUnion, a temporal multi-sensor dataset built from Carbon Mapper plume reports and matched S2, Landsat 8/9 (L8/9), EMIT, and Sentinel-5P (S5P) observations. Built on MethaneUnion, MethaneFuse learns from heterogeneous satellite observations under partial sensor availability without requiring complete four-sensor measurements.
  MethaneUnion expands usable coverage from 3,211 valid S2-matched plume cases to 8,981 reported plume cases with multi-sensor observations. At the representative 480 m setting, MethaneFuse achieves 84.87 F1 and 93.62 AUROC, improving over the strongest baseline by 5.65 F1 and 8.30 AUROC points while reducing false positives by 8.19 points. Sensor-availability experiments show that MethaneFuse improves detection when S2 is available and transfers plume knowledge to L8/9, EMIT, and S5P when S2 is unavailable. These results demonstrate the value of learning from incomplete heterogeneous sensor observations for practical methane plume detection.

</details>


### [31] [LogiScope-VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Logistics Hazard Identification in Industrial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09790)
*Hanjing Zhou,Mingze Yin,Ying Lian,Jun Ma,Chang-Yu Hsieh,Yanbing Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LogiScope-VQA，一个面向工业仓储与物流场景的大规模多模态评测基准，用于检验主流LMMs的专家级危险感知、理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业仓储场景对LMMs的部署要求其具备人类专家级的危险感知与推理能力，但真实工业数据稀缺且受商业条款限制，阻碍了模型进步，因此亟需构建贴近真实物流作业的评测基准。

Method: 构建LogiScope-VQA，包含2,476张图像、2,918段主要来自真实物流园的视频，以及10,274条由人工标注者精心整理并验证的VQA；基于18类核心对象与20种风险类型，设计39个子任务，覆盖工业要素感知、仓储知识理解、潜在风险推理三大主题。同时引入动态思考预算配置和双维度风险偏差分析。

Result: 大量实验表明，即使GPT-5.5、Gemini-3.1-Pro、Claude-Opus-4.7等强大专有模型也相对人类表现存在显著差距；感知、理解与推理联合整合用于危险识别构成独特挑战，仍有很大提升空间；并揭示了阻碍LLMs实际部署的普遍安全偏差问题。

Conclusion: LogiScope-VQA为工业物流场景下LMMs的危险识别能力提供了系统评测平台，凸显当前模型与人类专家水平的差距，并指出安全偏差等关键问题；数据集以CC BY-NC-SA 4.0许可公开。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) large-scale deployment in industrial warehouse settings specifically necessitates that models exhibit human-expert-level hazard-oriented perception, understanding, and reasoning capabilities. However, the scarcity of real industrial data, tightly coupled to commercial terms, significantly hampers further advancement. To bridge this gap, we curate LogiScope-VQA to investigate the practical applicability of mainstream LMMs in real-world logistics operations. LogiScope-VQA comprises 2,476 images and 2,918 videos primarily sourced from real-world logistics parks, along with 10,274 VQAs meticulously curated and validated by human annotators. Grounded in 18 core objects and 20 risk types, we devise 39 subtasks aligned with three principal themes: industrial element perception, warehouse knowledge understanding, and potential risk reasoning. Furthermore, we incorporate dynamic thinking-budget configurations and dual-dimensional risk bias analyses to elucidate the properties of LMMs. Extensive experiments unveil that even powerful proprietary models, including GPT-5.5, Gemini-3.1-Pro, and Claude-Opus-4.7, exhibit a significant gap relative to human performance. The unique challenge of jointly integrating perception, understanding, and reasoning for hazard identification poses substantial headroom for further improvement on LogiScope-VQA. We additionally reveal the pervasive security bias issue that impedes LLMs' practical deployment in real-world settings. The industrial dataset is publicly available under the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 license.

</details>


### [32] [Freezing of Gait Prediction Under Spatial Occlusion: An IMU-Supervised Cross-Modal Distillation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09826)
*Chandan Biswas,Aryan Singh,Anabik Pal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨模态子空间蒸馏框架，将IMU的精度与视频的便捷性结合，实现无需穿戴设备的冻结步态（FOG）检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频法在连续原地转身任务中因下肢严重自遮挡导致姿态估计精度下降；IMU法虽不受视觉遮挡影响，但需牢固佩戴且难以在临床/实验室外部署，成本高。

Method: 跨模态子空间蒸馏：从预训练的运动学oracle中提取不变隐空间拓扑，作为结构监督信号指导非编码视觉架构训练；双流视觉模型对骨骼图节点与连续空间像素进行概率融合，当关节跟踪置信度下降时动态将依赖转移至未受遮挡的像素边界。

Result: 在公开的多模态帕金森病患者连续360°转身序列数据集上评估，应用感觉边界拓扑显著降低了跟踪评估熵；约束优化表明在零穿戴推理环境下可实现高精度的FOG预测边界。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态蒸馏融合IMU精度与视频便捷性，可缓解单模态FOG检测的局限，在无穿戴设备条件下实现高精度冻结步态预测。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder characterised by gradual deterioration of movement control. Automated freezing-of-gait (FOG) detection supports the objective assessment of gait-related motor impairment. Two common approaches are used for FOG prediction: (i) analysing video recordings of the patient's movements and (ii) analysing data collected using inertial measurement unit (IMU) wearable sensors attached to the patient's lower limbs. Video-based approaches may suffer detection errors during continuous turning-in-place tasks because the lower limbs undergo substantial geometric self-occlusion, degrading pose-estimation accuracy. IMU-based approaches are generally less affected by visual occlusion; however, they are difficult to deploy outside clinical or laboratory settings, as the sensors must be attached securely and remain in place throughout the assessment. Motivated by this, we propose a cross-modal subspace distillation framework to mitigate the limitations of unimodal FOG detection by combining IMU accuracy with video-based practicality. We extract invariant latent topologies from a pre-trained kinematic oracle to structurally supervise a non-encoded visual architecture during training. To resolve periods of severe spatial occlusion, a dual-stream visual model probabilistically fuses skeletal graph nodes and continuous spatial pixels, dynamically shifting reliance to uninterrupted pixel boundaries as joint tracking confidence drops. Evaluated against a public, multi-modal sequence dataset of Parkinson's individuals executing continuous $360^\circ$ turns, empirical results demonstrate that applying sensory boundary topologies strictly mitigates tracking evaluation entropy. Our constrained optimisation confirms that highly precise FOG prediction bounds can be achieved over zero-wearable inference environments.

</details>


### [33] [Layerwise Tunable Lifting Scheme for the Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09827)
*Abdumannon Yovkochov,An Le,Sungbal Seo,You-Suk Bae,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一族可调提升方案（LS-LayLatt），包含低通、高通及顺序式三种调谐策略，基于格型提升结构保证可逆性与稳定性，并在图像分类与异常检测任务上验证其性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双正交小波滤波器组在设计上需要在可调性与可逆性/稳定性之间取得平衡。作者希望提出既具参数可调能力、又能在任意参数取值下保持可逆与稳定的提升方案，以提升下游视觉任务的适应性。

Method: 提出三种提升策略：低通调谐（LS-LayLatt-LP）、高通调谐（LS-LayLatt-HP），以及联合自适应低频与高频分支的顺序式提升方案（LS-LayLatt-Sequential）。所有设计均基于格型（lattice-based）提升结构，从结构上保证提升函数在任意参数取值下的可逆性与稳定性。

Result: 将所提方法集成到 ResNet-18 主干网络中，在 DTD 数据集上进行图像分类，并在 MVTec-AD 的榛子图像及私有 KRC102S 数据集上进行异常检测。实验结果表明在所有评测任务上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于格型提升结构的可调小波滤波器组方案在保持可逆性与稳定性的同时，能够有效提升图像分类与异常检测性能，具有较好的通用性与推广应用潜力。

Abstract: This work introduces a family of tunable lifting schemes for biorthogonal wavelet filter banks. We propose three lifting strategies: low-pass tuning (LS-LayLatt-LP), high-pass tuning (LS-LayLatt-HP), and a sequential lifting scheme that jointly adapts low- and high-frequency branches (LS-LayLatt-Sequential). All proposed designs are formulated using a lattice-based lifting structure, which guarantees invertibility and stability for arbitrary parameter values within the lifting functions. We evaluated the proposed methods by integrating them into a ResNet-18 backbone for image classification on the Describable Textures Dataset (DTD), as well as for anomaly detection on hazelnut images from the MVTec-AD dataset and private KRC102S dataset. Experimental results demonstrate consistent performance improvements across all evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [34] [SkNeXt enables topology-guided neuronal reconstruction from petabyte-scale microscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09832)
*Jiayi Ding,Hu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkNeXt是一个“拓扑优先”的神经元重建框架：先在低分辨率下生成紧凑SWC骨架并校对，再以骨架作为结构先验与空间索引，仅沿轨迹选择性读取高分辨率图像，使重建成本随神经元形态而非数据集总量扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨荧光与电子显微镜可在越来越大体积的脑组织上成像，但产生的TB–PB级数据使完整神经元重建在计算、数据搬运与人工校对方面代价过高，需要一种可扩展的重建范式。

Method: 不进行全体积密集处理：先将神经元形态转换为保留长程连通性的紧凑SWC骨架；将校对集中在稀疏神经树，先修正分支、连续性与连通性错误；把校正后的骨架作为持久结构先验以恢复精细形态并保持神经元身份与拓扑；同时把骨架作为空间索引进行选择性数据访问，仅沿重建轨迹取回高分辨率图像区域，跳过大部分背景与无信号体积。

Result: 使用SkNeXt，在单块GPU上于一周内从PB级小鼠脑超分辨荧光数据集重建神经元，且无需对整个成像体积做穷尽式密集推理。

Conclusion: 以拓扑/骨架为先的结构先验与索引机制，可显著降低I/O与计算开销，使大规模体积显微数据的神经元重建成本与神经元形态复杂度相关，而非与数据集总规模相关，从而提升PB级重建的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in high-resolution fluorescence and electron microscopy have enabled nanoscale imaging across increasingly large brain volumes, but the resulting terabyte- to petabyte-scale datasets make complete neuronal reconstruction prohibitively expensive in computation, data movement, and manual proofreading. Here, we present SkNeXt, a topology-first framework for scalable neuronal reconstruction from large volumetric microscopy datasets. Instead of densely processing entire image volumes, SkNeXt first converts neuronal morphology into compact SWC skeletons that preserve long-range connectivity. Proofreading is therefore focused on sparse neuronal trees, allowing branch, continuity, and connectivity errors to be corrected before high-resolution reconstruction. The corrected skeletons then serve as persistent structural priors for recovering detailed morphology while preserving neuronal identity and topology. Crucially, SkNeXt also uses neuronal skeletons as spatial indices for selective data access, retrieving high-resolution image regions only along reconstructed trajectories and bypassing most background and signal-free volumes. This substantially reduces I/O and computational overhead, allowing reconstruction cost to scale with neuronal morphology rather than total dataset size. Using SkNeXt, we reconstructed neurons from a petabyte-scale super-resolution fluorescence dataset of the mouse brain on a single GPU within one week, without requiring exhaustive dense inference across the complete imaging volume.

</details>


### [35] [Pretraining and Distillation Matter More Than Architecture Family for Label-Free Single-Cell Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09863)
*Philip Graemer,Giuseppe Di Caprio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在严格控制的LIVECell相差显微基准测试中，预训练（而非架构）是CNN与Transformer性能差异的主要来源；蒸馏是提升单细胞分类部署性能的更有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微图像无标记单细胞分类的最佳深度学习架构尚无定论，已有基准对CNN与Transformer给出相互矛盾的结论，且常受父图像泄漏、优化与评估协议不一致等因素干扰，难以区分架构、预训练等各因素的独立作用。

Method: 在LIVECell相差显微数据上构建受控基准：采用源图像不相交的训练/验证/测试划分以避免父图像泄漏，并对EfficientNet、ViT、EVA-02统一优化、数据增强与评估协议，从而解耦架构、预训练、微调、token化与蒸馏的影响。

Result: 预训练使macro-F1提升3-4个点，最小预训练模型优于最强从头训练模型；最佳预训练CNN与Transformer差距小于0.5个点。ViT-S/8优于ViT-S/16，并以1/4参数量媲美大4倍的ViT-B/16。EVA-02中的逐层学习率衰减反而降低性能。由教师委员会蒸馏得到的EfficientNet-B0学生超越所有单独训练的骨干网络，包括EfficientNet-B5与EVA-02教师。

Conclusion: 严格的预训练与评估控制是解读生物医学架构基准的关键；相比单纯选择架构，知识蒸馏可能是实现实用单细胞分类更有效的路径。

Abstract: Choosing a deep learning architecture for label-free single-cell classification remains an open question, with microscopy benchmarks reporting conflicting conclusions about CNNs versus transformers.
